Heads Held High#

band: the Berenfaer Squads

Storyteller’s account of the events leading to the rise of the Berenfaer in the early 49th century

Arc I: Zolkar#

4817-02-05#

01 | 2025-05-24 | Mission 00: Agora#

wherein a ragtag band forms
within the spark that lit the Zolkarsdawn Agora in Elestair ablaze,
moving swiftly to burn down the pier where the slaver crews were disembarking to suppress them,
miraculously surviving the exchange in the fiery waters and making their way to the Propylaea,
where they joined the uprisen slaves and the Berenfaer in a bold charge to claim their freedom

4817-02-11#

02 | 2025-06-04 | Mission 01: Pagoda#

wherein the Arson Squad
keep busy with organising the growing unrest in the streets of Elestair,
and escape the guard in a heated chase halfway across the city alongside the Kommissar,
before embarking on a magic carriage northbound towards a lead

4817-02-20#

wherein the Arson Squad
infiltrate a half-built Sumrilan pagoda infested with a Bloodscale kobold kult,
traversing trapped rooms and rotting floors to nearly perish at a kobold greatpick’s edge,
and ultimately leave the burning temple not with a retrieved hostage but a vague research question

4817-02-21#

05 | 2025-06-29 | The Streets of Estair#

wherein the Arson Squad
arrive in Estair and stake out a shady kobold in a worker’s tavern,
tracking him down to an old manor before backing away to brief with Teucer,
and when yet another Berenfaer agent is kidnapped within the city, they set out to save him

4817-02-22#

03 | 2025-06-18 | Mission 02: Triphthalm pt.1#

wherein the Tunnel Squad
enter the last Karadoshan sewer lair undernearth the Agora,
and are met with the trapmaking ingenuity of the resident kobolds

04 | 2025-06-23 | Mission 02: Triphthalm pt.2#

wherein the Tunnel Squad
hold the tunnel crossroads by grease and fire and shield,
leaving it strewn with the heavy corpses of ogres and owlbears, pushing ever deeper

06 | 2025-10-03 | Mission 02: Triphthalm pt.3#

wherein the Tunnel Squad
face off against a fearsome troll bouncer and her boar,
and despite mounting wounds push on to confront the boss of the dungeon,
a kholo shapeshifter calling himself Kharoon,
who dies before he can complete his mysterious work on an ancient Karrokhi mural, taking Keryneios with him

07 | 2025-10-23 | Mission 03: Hermitage pt.1#

wherein the Arson Squad
part ways with the Owlet Squad where the tracks of Friar’s abductors diverge,
and come upon an ancient hermitage to Malahir,
bypassing the lounging slurks and wiping the floor with the Figleaf bandits

09 | 2025-11-29 | Mission 03: Hermitage pt.2#

wherein the Arson Squad
are joined by the dwarf vanguard Harnock
as they delve deeper into the hermitage,
fighting bandit witches and small-fry demons
through the kitchens and narrow corridors to the library

10 | 2025-12-10 | Mission 03: Hermitage pt.3#

wherein the Arson Squad
advance through the corridors to the animal pens,
where they put a stop to a demonic ritual upon a wolf,
taking a key from the witch ritualist,
with which they open the inner sanctum of the hermitage in bad shape
and rescue Friar from the clutches of the last Fig-Leaf bandits

11 | 2025-12-19 | Mission 03: Hermitage pt.4#

wherein the Arson Squad
rejoined by the exhausted Owlet Squad,
take point in drawing a throng of ghouls out of the hermitage crypt,
with Harnock choosing to retire after the planned retreat is held up;
when discovered correspondence reveals that slaver mercenary companies are on route to Ilistair,
the agents must decide how to consolidate their positions

12 | 2025-12-30 | the road to Estair#

wherein the Arson Squad
alongside the Kommissar and Friar
are waylaid by the last Figleaf bandit and his amphisbaena
on the road back to Estair

13 | 2026-01-12 | Friar returns to Estair#

wherein the Arson Squad
enter Estair through the shallow canal waters near the Aldwindpoort,
and accompany Friar as he joins his flock briefly
before throwing the guards out of his chapel and departing north,
leaving the squad to stir up the people of Estair

4817-02-23#

08 | 2025-11-06 | Mission 04: Hightown#

wherein the Jackal Squad
infiltrate a mercantile complex in the Elestari Upper City through guarded checkpoints and rancid sewers
to eavesdrop on a meeting Governor Forster and Guard-Commander DePaars,
before escorting two noble sons and one servant maid back down in the districts of rebellion

14 | 2026-01-17 | the streets of Estair#

wherein the Arson Squad
split up to investigate the social dynamics of Estair,
in the piers where dockworkers haul and the slums where paupers huddle

15 | 2026-01-22 | the streets of Estair#

wherein the Arson Squad
blend and distract and swim their way across the city towards the Fjoertoer lighthouse and Drakkartor tower on the rocky coast, where they briefly meet with the eccentric gnome tinkerer Doktoor

16 | 2026-01-29 | Ald Arkhondijk#

wherein the Arson Squad
rejoin the Kommissar on the foothills towards the Ald Arkhondijk
and procure a scroll of invisibility to better enable them to sneak in and provide him backup in his risky bid to sway the kobolds

17 | 2026-02-05 | the streets of Elestair#

wherein the Jackal Squad
wind through the portside neighbourhoods of Elestair to get to the old tannery,
and engage in a verbal debate in a portside plaza scaffold ’ere the guard arrives

18 | 2026-02-07 | portside plaza gallows#

wherein the Jackal Squad
regroup back in the Lower Commons and attempt to retrace their course towards the old tannery,
but when met with the sight of gallows in the square and Guard-Commander DePaars declaring s ummary executions,
they intervene and spill more guardsman blood in order to rescue young dissident Gilles from the noose

19 | 2026-02-09 | Laagfenirck’s bog pt.1#

wherein the Arson Squad
witness Kommissar detained at spearpoint by the kobolds the moment he sets foot in the Ald Arkhondijk,
and discreetly follow them to the swampy lake banks where the young dragon Laagfenirck appears to render judgement;
as a terrified Kommissar attempts to make his point, a gunshot rings in the air

20 | 2026-02-12 | the streets of Elestair#

wherein the Jackal Squad
retreats to the nearest barricade to lick their wounds, afore Stroopwaffel finds them while making the rounds, and bids them accompany her back to the Agora, where they debrief the Berenfaer ringleaders;
after Steelshade reveals that the governor has invited the garrison into the city,
the ringleaders retire to plan while the squad regroups in the Propylaea where they are greeted by a grateful Gilles

21 | 2026-02-18 | jailbreak at the old tannery#

wherein the Jackal Squad,
joined by the quartermaster Sarakas,
are tasked to confidentially distribute signal flares to the barricade agents in anticipation for the coming of the garrison, on their way to the old tannery where they quickly dispatch the torturers and free their victims

22 | 2026-03-01 | Laagfenirck’s bog pt.2#

wherein the Arson Squad
are engaged in battle by the young dragon Laagfenirck
who precludes most kobold support in an effort to disprove the Kommissar’s populist overtures,
but ends up fleeing, defeated and gravely wounded;
in his battle rage, Ruatha rushes into the waters to claim the dragon’s head, but is knocked out by his tail instead, and is dragged down to the marshy depths of the lake

23 | 2026-03-10 | Laagfenirck’s bog pt.3#

wherein the Arson Squad
converse with the kobolds and mourn Ruatha’s passing on the banks of Qocytias,
and the Kommissar reflects on what went wrong and right in a self-critical session
on the road back to Estair; he renames the party as the “Dragoon Squad”
before taking off to pitch his programme to the kobolds, leaving the party
to knock on Doktoor Drakkartor’s door for supplies

4817-02-24#

24 | 2026-03-12 | the streets of Estair#

wherein the Dragoon Squad
rest, heal, and scout out the neighbourhood for their next move,
before the Kommissar returns along with Reyna bearing the news of a new secured alliance
and declares the squad experienced enough for him to pass the helm,
when they receive a missive from Ilistair containing an additional message hidden in invisible ink

25 | 2026-04-02 | seeking out Briar#

wherein the Dragoon Squad
head north, accompanied by Kommissar and shadowed by his kobold brigade,
to seek out the advice of Briar, the ghoran druid revealed in Teucer’s misleading dispatches;
upon learning of this context, Briar mentions a cutter boat moored nearby that would offer a faster and safer route to Ilistair,
and the squad sets out to claim it accompanied by the sailing-enthusiast redcap Haark

26 | 2026-04-05 | Hydra pt.1#

wherein the Dragoon Squad
lure out the Hydra lurking in the misty river coast, which lunges for them but quickly recedes back into the frigid riverbed when the cliffs prove too steep and the punishment too severe;
the squad vanguard rushes for the boat to pursue, but not all can steer clear of the surface and the beast below, and soon slippery rocks precipiate frantic swimming and blood in the water

27 | 2026-04-18 | Hydra pt.2#

wherein the Dragoon Squad
regroup on dry ground and haul the cutter by the ropes, while Grenzo moves on board to suppress the Hydra; when the beast reemerges to devour him, it is met with a missile barrage from the Kommissar’s kobold reinforcements, and recedes into the dark waters as the squad sets sail for Ilistair, their training complete

28 | 2026-05-08 | the Battle of Elestair pt.1#

wherein the Jackal Squad
are roused before daybreak to assist wherever they can in the barricades against the incoming garrison forces

29 | 2026-05-30 | the Battle of Elestair pt.2#

wherein the Jackal Squad
rush towards the Swinndorf barricade only to meet its defenders midway, and hobble the advance of the garrison footmen and mounties long enough for the dissenters to escape

30 | 2026-06-18 | the Battle of Elestair pt.3#

wherein the Jackal Squad
rejoin Tulip at the Guilders’ Row barricade
and push back the first wave of incoming garrison forces

31 | 2026-07-17 | the Battle of Elestair pt.4#

Wrath of the Righteous#

band: the Crystalline Crusaders

An account of the War in Olgizmar
chronicled through the eyes of:

  • Syr Gwynne An Daragh
    Knight Hospitaller

and her companions:

  • Dante Atonmeer
    Elven Exemplar
  • Soraelis
    Battle Cleric of Yerastu,
  • Lashlath
    Fungal Ballerina

Chapter 00 - Prologue#

4817, summer#

Incipit.
’Twas not in the least how I expected things to unfold, but may it be.

Stumbled on a lead in Ekteron, right after my arduous pilgrimage to the Four Winds Monastery.
’Twas only proper to follow it, for I’ve had perhaps indulged myself thus too much.

The Wolf Legion was still anchored on the coast.
Lord Hida graciously arranged for me to be discreetly ferried to the shores of Olgizmar.
His heart still aches over the Realms, and though he knows not the details of our quest, he understands why the Everbryght roam.

Once in Olgizmar though, I got myself captured by the magisters;
foolishly enough that I shall not recount it.
Turns out, nonetheless, there was indeed a curious mineral discovery in this godsforsaken isle.
But not morghonljucht. Cold iron.

Chapter 01 - Cold Iron#

4817, late autumn#

01 | 2024-11-15#

Imprisoned, without due process, in the Vaelkith Correctional Facility mines.
Soraelis, Dante, and Lashlath have been here for three.

Ο επιστατης Augustus Vaelkith δε δειχνει σημασια στο ποστο του.
Προ ημερων καποιος ηρθε και τον προειδοποιουσε, τον απειλουσε πως δεν ειχαν ιδεα οι μαγιστροι τι καναν εκει και οτι προκαλουν την καταστροφη, πως δεν ξερουν με τι μπλεκουν και οτι επρεπε να φυγουν αμεσα.

The mining golems were sent forward nonetheless to undertake the heavy duty drilling, followed by mages that stabilised the mining shafts.
Then the labourers went in, for more manual processing and refinement, and to sniff out cold iron veins. That was us and about 60 more prisoners, comprised of undesirables and low-priority death-row inmates, made to waste away in the dark.

Kαποιοι σκληροπετσοι ειχαν κανει συμμορια:

  • the Quarterers
    Οσοι περνανε καποιες δοκιμασιες και μπαινουνε στη συμμορια, το ενα τεταρτο της δουλειας τους την κανουν αλλοι.
    Their chief is an orc tiefling by the name of Bolg.

Soraelis had stoically complied with the extra work;
Gwynne & Dante had come to blows with them instead.

The mines are connected with the surface through a portal.
Every week 5-6 guards with bracelets came to check, bring supplies in and take ore crates out.

4817, Graymist 12th#

Μας καλει ο Augustus Vaelkith.
A clockwork golem worker stopped responding in the depths; he wants us to scout out, track it down and drag it out.
The guards are unwilling to do it themselves.

’Tis then bound to be
quite hazardous. And we are
more expendable.

Given back our gear.

My naginata
and blade of the Everbryght
still there. Sweet relief.

The inmates look on curiously as we pass and head into the tunnels.

Cold Iron Depths#

Trudging in the dark with Gwynne’s halo lighting the path.
We find the golem wrecked, with a great depression on its side.
A stone in the same shape lies nearby — probably what took it out.

A breach on the cavern walls reveals broken brickwork.
Soraelis casts Light on a stone and throws it;
a segment of the brickwork suddenly animates and crushes the stone in flight.
After a brief pause, Lashlath casts Light on a stone and throws it.

Only a fool would repeat the same thing twice and expect different results. Or perhaps Rowena.

The floating brickwork likewise crushes the second stone in the air and turns, aggravated, towards Gwynne.

For the record, good sir, whatever you are, I cast no stone. I kept my light upon my crown.

⚔ Siege Shard

We advance to the animated brickwork, shields up.
’Tis a Siege Shard, imbued with strong feelings of a battle, visions of which blow through us as it strikes and shrieks.
Like a restless ghost it will return to life after destruction; it needs to be put at rest.
Lashlath’s dazing spells confound it, and it finally falls by Gwynne’s hand.

Gwynne
— kneels by its remains for words of repose.

Be at peace.
The siege that haunted thee
        Is over; so much so,
        That it has nearly
        Passed out of memory.
By the grace of the Observer,
    I consign thee to rest.

Σκοτεινο δωματιο διαφαινεται μεσα απο ανοιγμα, εκει που ηταν το Siege Shard.
A banner, worn and tattered, a bit of red drape, stale air.

We enter an ancient hall, lined with statues of outsiders, each pair opposed in the cosmic sense. Angel, devil, vethian, fey, agathion, demon, kenemi, fire elemental, water elemental, dragon, elf.
At the end lies a mural, depicting all the creatures, hands raised towards a big crystal in the centre.

  • Ancient Mural
    This place is a message… and part of a system of messages… pay attention to it!
    This place is not a place of honor… no highly esteemed deed is commemorated here… nothing of value is here.
    What is here was dangerous and repulsive to us. This message is a warning about danger.
    The danger is in a particular location… it increases towards a center.
    The danger is still present, in your time, as it was in ours.
    The danger is to the soul, and it can kill.
    The form of the danger is an emanation of energy.
    The danger is unleashed only if you substantially disturb this place physically. This place is best shunned and left uninhabited. Unless all is lost to the demons, do not choose this option.
    Never again.

Another mural lies in another room, too damaged to decipher; the symbol of the siege
can be made out in the dust, as can recent footprints.

Following the tracks we come upon Bolg and three more Quarterers.
Soraelis talks loudly and they hear us.
Despite our previous confrontations, Bolg speaks civilly.
They were offered the same deal by Augustus, and rolled down here to scout out the place. There was a door that closed behind them, and a bit of loot they found.

We warily part ways with them, and push on to explore a dark corridor.
An undead suddenly rises inside the side room.

DEATHLESS PRIEST OF AMEHAN
Why are you here? this place was supposed to never be breached.

02 | 2024-12-12#

⚔ ️Undead Priests

Amehanite summons more undead priests from the side room.
We hold the entrance and then lunge to him, shields raised.
Gwynne hacks 3 crits through the priests.

Soraelis finds spent Wyrsa crystals in the corner.
Illusory wall hides adamantine door with 2 romboid alcoves.
In 3 rooms lie 3 symbols of Voolthow, Amehan, and Neverborn.

Voices rise from behind the door: the Quarterers.
Ψαχνουν ενα δευτερο κρυσταλλο και μιλανε για εσοχες, σαν αυτες που μολις ειδαμε στην αδαμαντινη πυλη. Θαρρουν πως εχουμε πεθανει.
Τους συνανταμε στο διαδρομο, κρατουν ενα κρυσταλλο που εκπεμπει φως, και μετα απο αλλη μια διπλωματικη στιχομυθια κινουν προς την αλλη κατευθυνση.

We move to the hall they just came out of, two great statues lie within, with corresponding inscriptions in empyrean and diabolic.

  • Empyrean: “I open at the closing”
  • Diabolic: “I close at the opening”

Soraelis muses about “darkness from light and light from darkness ”and a profound rumble shakes the depths.
As we turn to leave, we witness a pair of giant constructs hunting the Quarterers towards us. We flee around the side rooms where the undead priests were, letting the constructs pass us by, and keep running in the opposite direction.

On the narrow hallway we come across a crypt of Grand Priest Thorgrim of Yerastu.
Just a bit further, a tomb of an Archpriest of Sergonial.

The definition of antithesis, is this place; it insists on putting cosmological opposites side by side so, that the centerpiece must either be a shrine to absolute balance or really, really dangerous.

At the end of the corridor, we emerge into a hall with a dwarven statue, and then another lined with symbols of Nahema, Reszek, and Yerastu.
A mummified cleric of Reszek rises to address us while the Quarterers flee. Our hands are stayed by the sheer oxymoron of his existence.

DEATHLESS PRIEST OF RESZEK
The negative binding has been destroyed.
By disturbing this place you have endangered the world itself.
We have an hour at most to react; you must take the gauntlet.
This thing in the inner chamber, many have wished for and has been shut there so none may possess it.
It now must be sent to another dimension.
I will call the priests, but one mortal must take the gauntlet.
When things are ready, you must then throw it into the portal.

We rush down the hallways, passing by sarcophagi of rising dwarven priests, with no time to dwell on the blame.
Finally coming to a stop in a secluded room at the end, over a dark altar we witness a gauntlet that hovers, made of void glass.
Soraelis deems Gwynne the most suited to carry this artifact towards the portal to the Fortress of the Just that the undead priests shall open.
Gwynne crosses herself and takes up the gauntlet, and immediately all magic is suppressed on her by its power.

The Quarterers suddenly emerge from around the corner and throw an acid flask at her.

BOLG
“Sorry boys, business is business.”

⚔ ️️️️the Quarterers

Grease is cast underneath the feet of Gwynne and Dante, as the two scrappers Ennia and Bolg move up to shove us into it.
The caster Grick on the back lobs a Flaming Sphere to torment Soraelis.
The alchemist Jonathan throws one grenade after the other.
Gwynne holds her ground against hits and grenades, a Force Barrage fizzles against the gauntlet. Ennia keeps trying to knock her down in vain.
A bitter, prolonged scuffle finally ends with the Quarterers on the back foot, and breaking into a rout when Bolg falls.
We run down 3 of them; the alchemist escapes behind a door that the undead priest warned us to ignore.

03 | 2024-12-15#

Return to the undead priests assembling, Dante and Soraelis backtrack to retrieve one of the crystals from Bolg’s body.
Βρισκουν τον αλχημιστη κοκκαλωμενο στην πορτα, κατι μεσα του ειπε λεει να μην κουνησει μεχρι να καταλαβει τι συμβαινει. Οταν μια φωνη στα σκοτεινα οντως ρωτα αγριεμενα “με ποιον μιλας”, κλεινουν την πορτα και γυρναν.

Καθως οι ιερεις κλιμακωνουν, τρεχουμε προς την πυλη με τις δυο εσοχες.
Over there stand Augustus Vaelkith, a strange horned lady, and a pair of golem hulks.

The Horned Magister playfully scolds Augustus over being hasty; he had invited her over finding something big and this is not it.
As we enter the room, Augustus tries to make a show for some progress and nervously prods us to cooperate, yet is met with our disdain after the Quarterers’ attack, apparently on his orders.
The Horned Magister is amused by the altercation, and hungrily observes the gauntlet artifact on Gwynne’s arm. Μας προσπερνα και παει να μιλησει με τους ιερεις σαν ακουει πως μας στειλαν εδω, ετσι απλα.

GWYNNE
Who are you?

STRANGE WOMAN
All in good time, sweetie.

Defeated, Augustus slids down to the floor and curses his day.
Περα απο την πυλη, σαν ανοιξει με τα δυο πετραδια στις εσοχες της, κειται τεραστιος βωμος πανω στον οποιο ενας σπασμενος κρυσταλλος αιωρειται τρεμαμενος.
Γυρω του πλεουν θραυσματα υλης, σαν εκρηξη που παγωσε στον χρονο.

Gwynne slowly paces through the time-stopped field, protected by the gauntlet, and grabs the crystal from its mid-air suspended state; it subtly cracks on her touch, and time begins flowing again as the field around the alter fades away.

We rush back to where the deeathless priests were opening a portal to the Fortress of the Just, only to witness a scene of carnage: most of the priests lay strewn across the floor, the Reszekine barely standing while the Horned Magister, unscathed, addresses him flanked by her golems.

STRANGE WOMAN
Get busy answering or get busy dying, darling.

DEATHLESS PRIEST OF RESZEK
The artifact is not meant for you. Or anyone.

STRANGE WOMAN
You would have it hidden somewhere, forgotten, doing nothing; I would rather put it to use, to the benefit of Olgizmar, to push against the demon plight. I must have it.

⚔ the Horned Magister and her 2 Golems

The golems advance on Gwynne, activate defences, and pummel her raised shield.
The Deathless Reszekine casts Divine Wrath, frying one of the golems, but the Horned Magister phases out and dodges the spell entirely.
Glimpsing the portal at the end of the room, Gwynne makes a desperate dash for it through the Golems but is knocked over by one. Exhausted by her wounds and the lack of magical healing ever since she put on the gauntlet, she drops on the floor and the crystal escapes her grasp.

Time seems to slow.
The Horned Magister’s eyes widen, and she immediately teleports away.

Uttering a perfectly cut expletive, I might add.

Απο το ραγισμενο πετραδι εκτινασσεται εκτυφλωτικο φως.

  • Vision

Ξυπνω στην επιφανεια.
Ουρανος κατακοκκινος, γεματος καπνους.
Το γαντι ειναι ακομα στο χερι μου.
Tall, crumbling, alien structures tower in the distance.
The ground beneath my feat is blackened as if by an explosion.
A giant crater gapes ahead.
A silhouette stands against the fiery horizon light, of red skin and mixed, mismatched artificial tissue.

V2
I have a question to ask; why make something aligned with destruction to save the world?
This is the only way it could have ended.
War no longer needed its ultimate practicioner. It had become a self-sustaining system.
Man was crushed under the wheels of a machine created to create the machine to crush the machine.
Samsara of cut sinew and crushed bone.
Death without life.
Null ouroboros.
All that remained is war without reason.
A cold tower of steel.
A machine built to end war is always a machine built to continue war.
You reached out for god, and you fell.
None were left to speak your eulogy.
No final words, no concluding statement.
No point.
Perfect closure.

GWYNNE
Am I dead?

V2
No.

GWYNNE
If not, then how did I get here? What is this?

V2
These are the bloodied skies of the Final War, where the world feeds on itself. Far at the end of your world’s future, yet you witness it by virtue of the divine eye that shattered in your hands.

GWYNNE
The eye of a god… Of Veth?

V2
Good. You’re picking up fast.

GWYNNE
Who are you? What are you?

V2
I was one of those who held Veth’s Eye to you;a piece of it is lodged in your heart.
I am designated Unit V2, created for the final war; and now, your guide.
Perhaps you may help this boring future not come to pass.

Ανακταμε τις αισθησεις μας και οι τεσσερις σε αμμωδη παραλια, διπλα σε ναυαγιο αρχαιο που ξεβρασε η θαλασσα.
Ο τυπος που ειχε ερθει και προειδοποιουσε τον επιστατη να κλεισει τα ορυχεια στεκεται απο πανω μας.

Gwynne looks to her punctured breastplate: a shard of the Eye of Veth is indeed lodged deep in her sternum.
Soraelis and Dante are also pierced with shards of their own.
The fungal leshy Lashlath has taken on aquatic coral features, and the creature addresses her.

THE FIRST TO SPEAK
Primordial One.
We many are in disarray.
We many have no beginning and no ending.
The Primordial One sacrificed himself to save you, has absorbed the blast, has transferred its conscience to you.
I am the first to speak.
[ … ]
Our creator gave us one task and duty: keep the demons out.
Η τελευταια οδηγια του Primordial One ηταν να σας σωσω.
[ … ]
Γνωριζω ο,τι γνωριζει το Σμηνος, αλλα το Σμηνος δεν γνωριζει και πολλα.

Dazed, Gwynne stands up and looks to the hand that wore the gauntlet.

’Tis now crude inside, creaking, ticking. Like a regeneration gone awry.
A replacement. The beginning of a likeness to the mechanical daemon.
Visions of the future, his time, under bloody skies: I am like this. All of me.
The horror. The horror.

Στον νυχτερινο ουρανο, το βορειο σελας παλλεται.

The First To Speak said that they were keeping the demons out.
Is that barrier broken? Did we do this?

Judging by the stars, we are lying on the southwest coast.
In the horizon, beyond the sandbanks, a city glows ablaze in the night.
It is Branstraz.

Grave is our sin. The horror.
Observer have mercy, for we acted not with reckless curiousity, but good faith: we sought to keep it from the magisters. From the moment they sniffed the cold iron, this was inevitable.
Nonetheless, we did this. And my hand…
I shall accept my penance. Help me make this right.
Observer. Lady Luna. Ancestor spirits. Have mercy.

Chapter 02 - City on Fire#

4817, Graymist 14th#

04 | 2025-01-09 | Entering Branstraz#

Branstraz has 3 gates: one to the harbour (that we can see also burning), one to our side, and one to the North, for which we must go around the city.

We opt for the latter and walk bearing NE.
Small oval things are levitating in the air without doing anything.
As we approach the city, we witness demonic silhouettes running around, setting fire to the suburbs, and advance to confront them.

⚔ ️a dozen Brimoraks

Loose arrows, but none find their marks, the flaming demons close the distance fast.
Lashlath conjures water to extinguish them.
One single-mindedly pursues civilian woman and slashes her back.
After repositioning to protect her and taking a few fiery hits, Gwynne shoves a Brimorak down into a nearby well.
Dante crits another into oblivion and cries havoc.
Soraelis lets out a healing burst, which by the grace of Yerastu burns radiant upon the demons.
Lashlath, still playing around their water vulnerability, breaks off a fire hydrant dousing everyone in the area.
The Brimorak that fell in the well has just about climbed up when Dante cuts off its hand, sending him back down in a watery grave.
The remaining Brimoraks spray their boiling blood. As Gwynne struggles to connect hits, Dante steps in and decapitates them with his greatsword.
Soraelis shoots down the last fleeing straggler.

Before we even can collect our breath, the civilian woman embraces Soraelis and kisses him; shocked, he tries to resist but she whispers in his ear, suggesting he save her from us. “Look around you, these are demons!”
Gwynne cries out “Succubus!”, prompting Dante to strike at her, revealing her true form while the sorcerous seduction is counteracted by Lashlath.

Short-sighted, impatient fiend.
To think that she was devious enough to orchestrate this deceit, only to blow her cover immediately.

Once downed, we find a scroll on her, written in Cthonic.

Pit-Lord Orgyssh’s missive
We do not yet know why this plane’s Teleportation magic is all messy. Better not risk it until we have figured out what is going on. Last thing we want is another wrong sending. We were extremely lucky we found a relatively undefended city instead of right into a fortified area.
Take as many demons as you need, try to find some mortals that seem capable while I keep the garrison occupied. Find what the hell is going on, and where to go from there. I know you can lure a mage or two with your charms.

Upon the insistence of Soraelis, who is a local, we forego collecting any valueables on-site to march on into the city.
Gwynne’s reconstructed hand makes a flashing obscene gesture when Soraelis’s back is turned.

Good grief, it acts on its own? This cannot stand for long.

As we forge onwards into the burning streets, we come across magister Thorbandim, a short gnome accompanied by a giant construct, dousing flames with a water hose.
Βλεποντας τον Soraelis λεγει του “εσυ εισαι το κωλοπαιδο που εσπασε το σπιτι μου!”.
Without caring to elaborate on that, he bids us take up water buckets and help him contain the blaze.

THORBANDIM
45 χρονια μαγιστρος και κατεληξα να κανω τον πυροσβεστη.
Χθες βραδυ εμφανιστηκαν 80 δαιμονες στα προαστια. Οι περισσοτεροι πολιορκουν την πολη.
Teleporting and sending magic are prone to catastrophic errors. Ενας συναδελφος που δοκιμασε teleport κατεληξε στην κολαση.
Avoid everything in the Conjuration school.
Something twisted must have occured, for magic to be this unstable for the past 3 days.
Οταν σκασαν οι δαιμονες, οι κατοικοι κατεφυγαν εντος των τειχων.
Αλλα η ασταθης μαγεια επιασε και τους δαιμονες με τα σωβρακα κατω.
Πριν 3 μερες σκασαν και 2-3 Vethians, λεγανε ψαχνουμε καποιους τυπους.

Μενουμε λιγο να περιορισουμε την πυρκαγια και μετα κινουμε προς την πυλη.
Καθως πλησιαζουμε, μια βολη απο το βαλλιστρο του πυργου χτυπαει το εδαφος μπροστα απο τον μαγιστρο.

THORBANDIM
Eγω ειμαι ρε ηλιθιε, δε βλεπεις το γκολεμ πισω?

05 | 2025-02-09 | Meeting the magisters#

We enter and head to the temporary headquarters of the magisterium.
The guards have barely slept.
Inside, magister Thorbandim introduces us to magister Kalix (an abjuration specialist) and magister Arya Spellsmith (a visiting colleague).

I freeze a bit and look at the latter with apprehension. The hair, the hat, and the alias cannot all be a coincidence.
It would be just like her to sneak into trouble thus, but not to be this careless with her cover.

The magisters acknowledge that we are capable enough to trust with aiding the city’s defence, and brief us into the situation developing mostly around the harbour district.
Kalix mentions that they had observed behavioural irregularities in the moon Veth.
They suggest 3 places we might hit:

  1. The demon headquarters in the Temple of Malahir.
  2. Magister Venthric Stroming’s house, suspected to contain something that could help us turn the tide.
  3. The Mage Academy

Magister Arya Spellsmith pulls me to the side to treat me for burns. Seems she got the hint.
Once out of earshot, I recite the first verse of the Everbright stanza to check if it’s really her. She completes it not, but recognises me nonetheless as one who knows.
Turns out she is but her body double, a deliberate false lead; she even panics about the desperate circumstances in a similar way.
Diversion and counter-intelligence. That’s much more like our litle rose.

The magisters spare us some weapon runes before we set out.
Gwynne unpacks her naginata blade and affixes it to a suitable pole, then asks for a mount.
They grant her a stallion that belonged to a merchant gone missing since the distaster struck. Gwynne and Lashlath soothe its spirits for the conflict that lies ahead.

If we are to keep evil at bay today,
I must make full use of my strengths.
Let my knight’s banner unfurl.
May the Watcher bless you, my noble steed; thy name shalt be Hector,
for your coming valiant service in the defence of your home.
Long may we ride.

Προχωρωντας στους δρομους αναμεσα σε μισοκατεστραμμενα οδοφραγματα και πτωματα, παρατηρουμε κατι να μιμειται ατσαλα ανθρωπινη κινηση.
Το πλασμα μας πλησιαζει, η μορφη του παρομοια με ενος παλιατσου.
Deranged and reveling in the carnage. An azuretzi vethian.

VETHIAN CLOWN
Επιτελους, ζωντανο κοινο! Εσυ, δεσποινις! Ποιο ειναι το αγαπημενο σου ζωακι?

GWYNNE
[ levelling her naginata against it ]
… Ο γρύψ.

Αμα τηι αποκρισηι της Gwynne, ο παλιατσος παει πανω απο ενα πτωμα brimorak, ξεριζωνει το δερμα του, το φορα, το φουσκωνει στην μορφη ενος γρυπα.
Απεγδυομενος το δερματινο μακαβριο μπαλονι της το δινει.
While she’s lost for words, Soraelis shoots the clown.
In response it takes his form upon itself and mimics him in a mocking pitch.

VETHIAN CLOWN
“Dominion of evil, I shall not!”

As we barely hold a snicker in, Soraelis furiously attempts to exorcise the clown away.
Dante steps in and lops off its head.
It promptly reappears behind Soraelis.

VETHIAN CLOWN
Tough crowd…

Exasperated, Soraelis demands we move on. We oblige, leaving the clown to its devices.

’Tis the first time I have been given a baloon;
        a little girl in me wistfully sighed.
    As I am human,
        my state is desperate for a small measure of joy.
    As I am angel…
now alas the day.

Across the neighbourhood streets we walk upon, the market stalls lie broken and burning. Demons lie afoot, sieging a particular house.

⚔ ️Large Babau with 2 brimorak

Gwynne ducks out of a fireball blast; the others are not so lucky.
The blaze is followed by impenetrable magical darkness.
As soon as Dante and Lashlath have stormed ahead, the 2nd Brimorak lobs another fireball past them into the Yerastites.
Sensing Soraelis felled by the blast Gwynne lays her hands to resuscitate him and charges the Babau through the darkness with her naginata leading.
Through the scuffle with the three demons, the Babau recoils in mental revulsion at every wave of divine healing surging from Soraelis, and realising it cannot break our frontline to get to him, tries to blink through; due to teleportation instability however, it instead reappears high above the adjacent house and falls down through its roof, helplessly perishing when the next healing goes out.

Hast thou ever been so comically evil that you survived crash-landing through a house, but then got so mad about someone receiving the comfort of holy vitality that you just died?

The demons, it turns out, had been raiding a jeweler’s store; the brimoraks in particular, carrying away packs of silverware.
Soraelis runs to the nearby house of a friend of his.

06 | 2025-02-17 | Venthric’s Mansion#

This Kyonin only opens his door after a trial of authenticity, wary of succubus impersonation.
His wife is with child and in her last month, so they dared not journey out of the house.
He says survivors might have made for the port, and that some friends have barricaded themselves in the sewers.

As twilight comes, we move on to the docks.
Corpses of humans, elves, and dwarves litter the streets.
Αναμεσα τους κοιτονται μαυροφορεμενοι τυποι, κοντα στα απομειναρια απο dretches και babau.
Assessing the battleground, Gwynne posits that the battle started from harbour gates and retreated towards the docks.
Most have wounds on the back, yet a vrock impaled on a pole shows the thieves’ ambush was rather potent; their gear is fine and, most tellingly, they are not human but some sort of vampire.

Κινησεις και πανικοβλητοι ψιθυροι στα σκοτεινα κινουν την προσοχη μας.
A lean person, heavily laden with ill-gotten loot, stammers and tries to stall before we call the other one out.
Finally the panic, try to distract with an imaginary vrock, and run.

⚔ ️2 Thieves

The miscreants throw grease on the stairs leading to docks, but Gwynne astride Hector jumps over the edge and rides one down; before he can yield, Soraelis shoots him dead.
Dante chases the other one, immobilising him by throwing his sword on top, that only the worthy may lift.

We pressure the thief into following us to the mage’s house.

Further ahead we spy a vrock surrounded by lesser demons: dretches, brimoraks, the works; we hide behind a fence and eavesdrop

VROCK
Δε γινεται να κανουμε κατι πλεον, διαταγες ξεδιαταγες.
Θελει να ριχνουμε πτωματα σε μια επαυλη εναντια σε ενα μαγο που δε ξερω αν ειναι καν σημαντικος.

Κοιταξτε τι θα γινει: δε λεμε τιποτα στον Orgyssh και αμα μας ρωτησουνε, λεμε οτι ο μαγος ειναι νεκρος.

Κατοπιν της ελεγειας του μαζευονται και φευγουν.
Η επαυλη εχει υποστει ζημια αλλα δεν καιγεται.

Μπαινοντας στον πολυτελη χωρο υποδοχης, ολα μοιαζουν αθικτα περα απο μια κηλιδα αιματος που πηζει κατω απο την πορτα στα δεξια.
Ανοιγοντας την, θωρουμε τη σορο μιας καμαριερας, μια μεγαλη τρυπα χασκει στο στηθος της.
We debate a while as to the cause.

The aftermath of a Death Clutch spell. Much too powerful to use on a simple servant.
I posit she was the unwitting victim of a magical trap, and commend her soul to the river of spirits.

Εντος του γραφειου του μαγιστρου, φθαρμενες καρεκλες πεσμενες κατω, παραθυρο ραγισμενο ωσαν καποιος να προσπαθησε να το σπασει.
Στο τραπεζι, διαφορες σημειωσεις και βιβλια. Σε μποτιλια, διατηρημενο σε φορμαλδευδη παραξενο θαλασσιο ον, και πλοιο σε μπουκαλι λιγο παραπερα.
Γοπες και μυρωδια απο ακριβα τσιγαρα. Ειχε ντουμανιασει καποιος εδω μεσα.
A selection of filters and elixirs:

  • Knockout drum
  • “Peace in Dreams” Tea

Επι χειρογραφου σε σπασμενα ακατανοητα draconic κειται ενας σφραγιδολιθος του Κυκλου των Zvennen.
The name of the magister: Venthric Stroming

Among the outgoing correspondence, the most recent letter is to Marcus Wolfheart, herder of a massive flock, asking for 85 tons of manure with lots of erased spelling mistakes; the next one says he cannot return to teaching, for there is a strange occurence in the sea, and he is searching for a druid that survived the original demonic invasion to consult.
Among the incoming letters stands out one replete with threats in red ink.
Soraelis gathers those, but directs we leave the rest.

Further around the corner we come across 3 charred corpses.
In an opulent lounge, a game of chess has been left unfinished before a buffet of fine wines.
Outside window we see the remains of a frozen brimorak.

On a piano lies some sheet music, that Lashlath plays by the windgrooves of its body instead.

Gwynne and Dante open the double door to the dance hall, only to be confronted with the sight of a massacre smelling of the sea: an amorphous grey mass of fish melted by acid.

Smee the thief we dragged along, has proved adept in navigating the magical protections and hazards.
Upon Gwynne’s tighter inquiry, he admits to having robbed 20 mage houses to date.
When we come across a dark blue ooze in the showers, horrified he bids us shut the door and move away.

We finally find the rest of the staff in the dining hall, fast asleep in the chairs around the long table, four of which are empty. The window is partly cracked, the bodies of demons strewn outside.

Four empty chairs for four dead servants that we found thus far. They woke, and startled by the demonic assault run through the halls to their doom.
Tutum sit iter tuum.

Note on the table
Αγαπητο υπηρετικο προσωπικο, λυπουμαι αλλα εχουμε εισελθει σε μιαν εκτακτη κατασταση κρισης. Για τη δικια σας προστασια σας ναρκωσα.
Εχω αφησει αρκετο φαι για μια βδομαδα, μη βγειτε για κανενα λογο προτου παρελθει ή εως οτου βγουμε απο την κατασταση κρισης.
Ευχαριστω για την κατανοηση.
Αν μετα απο μια βδομαδα δεν εχετε ακουσει νεα μου, ιδου οδηγιες για το πως να φυγετε.

We then explore the mage’s personal collection (containing among others an ancient selanaari blade, a cosmological model, and most importantly dragon eggs), his artisan’s workshop, the kitchen, and the servants’ quarters, before following a secret passage in a receded wall, leading to the basement.

In the cellar, we find a much more poor and plain workspace that has nonetheless been used much more over the past days.

On top of the pile of notes on the table, lies frantically written thrice “we must kill the ocean”
Απο κατω βρισκουμε καταγραφες προοδου για μια καλλιεργεια ενος οργανισμου που ονομαζει Algae, η οποια αν δεχθει αρκετα θρεπτικα συστατικα μπορει να επεκταθει παρα πολυ γρηγρορα και να φερει οικολογικη καταστροφη στερωντας φως απο το βυθο της θαλασσας.

We pace through the claustrophobic corridors to an underground cave run through by an artificial stream.
Soraelis maintains that we leave and go to the temple of Yerastu instead, and is confronted by Gwynne who wishes to see this lead through if it’s so important it would alter the conflict balance.

It is then that a ragged, half-mad magister Venthric emerges from the shadows; “interlopers” he speaks as he directs his malicious glare against us (and especially Lashlath).

24th Graymist 4817, the Church of Yerastu in Branstraz
Esteemed Sir/Syr Knight Commander

Sure as the light shines upon us, news of the grim events in Olgizmar must have rattled you on the continent.
Confrontation with the demons on a massive scale here seems inevitable, the country is in turmoil, and the Kenorex have moved in to stake a claim.
In the Branstraz Church of Yerastu, the archpriest Andromenes has been assassinated, and associates already advocate for a crusade to be called.
On my honour I testify, that the horrors afoot are world-ending enough that should such an avenue exist, the Hospitaller Order should not stand idle.
I humbly ask that you consult with what officers and officials you can reach, and remain at your disposal as, apparently, the only presence of our order in Olgizmar at this time; please advise.

In service and vigil,
Syr Gwynne Shizusdottir
Knight Hospitaller, Chapter of the West

To acolyte Soraelis. I write this to inform you what I found on your friend Stilgar. I’m afraid that it far more concerning than a waywards spirit.
Stilgar has become something the ancient archives call Palinthanatoi. It is the result of temporal magic messing with the fate of mortals: When one’s death is undone, but the soul is beyond Styx and cannot return. The result is something that resembles your friend, but is not, and is imprevious to all but the strongest magic. He will continue to repeat the last day of his life again and and again. I’m afraid there’s nothing to be done, not even I can do anything but contain him. If he is destroyed, he will simply manifest the next day.

This is an extremely dire portent. Whoever -or whatever- can create palinthanatoi can do it again. I will have to seek the knowledge of other temples on how to deal with such creatures. I will depart for the Bloodline, where from what i hear is still clear from the demons and held by the 6th army and Darrok troops. I will write you again when I have news, or if sending magic has restored itself.

Stay strong and may you keep the Balance.

Bránstraž
Walled city on the western coast of Olgizmar.
A former major base of the Gatewatch Order.

Crimson Prophet   folk#

revolutionary leader
purports to rally populace of Bránstraž against the magisters
bearer of a crystal

Dante   band#

Elven Exemplar
A bold swordsman reclaiming his memory.

Eye of Veth   item#

Ancient artifact with the ability to absorb, concentrate, and release vethian energy — such that a cantrip may be as strong as a Wish.

History (Kallindra, 4817-12-27)

  • Moved tangentially to the Material Plane but sinusoidally through time.
  • Seen around the Winter’s War in the hands of mage Ansiv
  • Kallindra led team of Olgizmari magisters to track its reappearance in Olgizmar and hold it to the Material Plane as a tool to save the country from itself
    • traitor in the ranks meddled for his own gains, destabilised the stasis field, leading to deaths of most participating mages
    • Kallindra called for help from the higher planes, Yerastu stepped in, arranged a solution, and sentenced her to be part of it
  • A mix from every major essence in the multiverse would contribute to the stabilisation of the Eye
    • Various paragons of their kind were called in. Some volunteered, some were pressured.
    • An underground crypt of cold iron was created with the Rod of Stone and Steel to house and hide the Eye. Deathless priests were called from the souls of departed priests to lie vigil.

Fragmentation
The crypt was eventually breached by miners. On the day of Vethfall, the band came upon the halls, attempted to help the Deathless Priests in containing the situation, but the Eye destabilised in the process and shattered.

  • Each fragment houses a single paragon entity, and can fuse with any creature coming into contact with it. The creature then may gain:
    • the unique powers stemming from the paragon entity, though this comes with the entity pushing their host to facilitate their aims
    • a curious form of regeneration wherein lost limbs are regrown as sovyrian crystal, a crystalline formation that could only be extracted from ley line nodes by a lost ancient elven race; none now know how to deal with save from a certain magister exile (Venthric, 4817-12-14)

The paragon entities, as depicted in an array of statues inside the halls of the crypt, are:

  1. Angel = Ronova, celestial of genesis
    hosted by Gwynne
  2. Devil = Mnemoch, the Hellsmith
    hosted by Soraelis
  3. Vethian = the Many, a hive mind of primordial algae hypothetically
    hosted by Lashlath
  4. Fey
  5. Agathion
  6. Demon = Unit V2, fiend of the end times
    hosted by Gwynne
  7. Kenemi = Deiktokrator, the Grand Arbiter
    hosted by Soraelis
  8. Fire elemental
    hosted by Lashlath
  9. Water elemental
  10. Dragon
  11. Elf = Kallindra, the Magistress
    hosted by Dante
  12. Undead = Kriemhilda, the Vampire
    hosted by Dante; crypt statue was missing or damaged

Fråddarth   faction#

The leading anti-isolationist magister circle in Olgizmar.

Gwynne   band#

Syr Gwynaëlle An Daragh
Knight-Hospitaller of Yerastu, Chapter of the Far West
Lance-Lieutenant of the 1st Griffon Cavalry, Sennarii Clans of Snow

“An Daragh” is Darroki dwarven for “the Oak”.
The title was bestowed on her by the dwarves of Strümeld when she paid them a visit during her Tarhelm pilgrimage, in recognition of her mother’s strength.
While Gwynne usually avoids introducing herself by her actual surname for that reason, “An Daragh” was vague enough to most not in the know that she gladly accepted the honour by adopting it.

Hector   band#

Gwynne’s noble steed
used to belong to a merchant before the demon attack

Horned Magister   folk#

Tiefling lady magister, markings of the Fråddarth circle. Encountered in the catacombs where the Eye of Veth lay.
She had her eyes on the Eye of Veth, and was powerful enough that nothing could scrape her in the struggle leading up to its shattering, shortly before which she teleported out of the dungeon.

She is thus probably the only one still alive who knows our implication in the story.

Jolly   folk#

azuretzi vethian in the form of a clown
stalks the band making crass clown routines

Kalix   folk#

magister of Branstraz

Kallindra   folk#

the Elf paragon entity in the Eye of Veth

Female elf magister of Selanaari descent, originally in the Fråddarth circle.
Born in 2877 (if her Kolix mansion password is to be taken at face value).

Kriemhilda   folk#

the Undead paragon entity in the Eye of Veth

Lashlath   band#

Fungal Ballerina
Bardic prodigy dancing in the breeze

Survived the mines by virtue of being a fungus leshy able to thrive underground.
The incident transformed it to a unique blend of mycoid and coral biology.
It is a living wood organ, producing melody by movement.

Orgyssh   folk#

balor at the head of the demon forces that attacked Bránstraž

  • perished at the hands of a magister

Redemptionists   faction#

Something between rebel troop and cult.
Led by the Crimson Prophet.
He says that rifts are divine punishment for the evils of the magisters and that the only way for them to close, is for the people to rise up and kill the Magisters.

They belong to different faiths;
many posit that it is a front for a demonic cult, or a tool of the magisters to further their ends.
Their renown has grown after the breaking of the barriers.

Ronova   folk#

the Angel paragon entity in the Eye of Veth
Ronova, Shade of the Heavenly Principles
one of the Shadows of the Equiliser

  • left behind to safeguard the balance
  • they appear infrequently, and only when everything else has failed; an ill omen

Smee   folk#

thief of the Bránstraž thieves’ guild
apprehended looting in the port district during the demon siege
redeemed himself by aiding the band in its defence

Soraelis   band#

Battle Cleric of Yerastu
A peerless archer letting loose holy fire.
A former resident of the Temple of Yerastu in Branstraz.
His divine font, beside granting respite to the wounded, also burns with empyrean radiance against the spawns of nether planes.

The First To Speak   folk#

Serviceman to the Primordial One (Veth), part of a group tasked to keep the demons out (or in).
Had visited Augustus Vaelkith to warn him to abandon the cold iron mines where the Eye of Veth lay.
Rescued the band from the waters once the mines were blown asunder upon its shattering.

Thorbandim   folk#

magister of Branstraz for 45 years
gnome wizard golemist

Unit V2   folk#

the Demon paragon entity in the Eye of Veth

A curious apparition, a hybrid of a daemon and machine.
Built for the Final War in the distant future, and clearly tired of it.

Vaelkith   faction#

Μια απο τις φατριες των μαγιστρων της Olgizmar.
Business dealings with the Fråddarth circle.

Ειχε την εργολαβια του Vaelkith Correctional Facility, και ορισε το ανεπιδεκτο βλασταρι της Augustus Vaelkith εκει ως επιστατη.

Venthric   folk#

magister of Branstraz
of the Zvennen magister circle
almost driven mad at the underbelly of his mansion, researching the Algae

Zvennen   faction#

magister circle of Olgizmar, based in Kolix

Handmaidens of Xenagos

Maenads are creatures of unbridled violence and decadence, maenads roam the world inviting others to join in on their debased revels. Though they can otherwise pass for humans, maenads appear bestial when raging or engaging in a bloody revel. They consume massive amounts of wine and food, cause fights, and tear their foes limb from limb. They control people’s minds, subconsciously inviting them to engage in their bloody festivities and fostering urges that lead to excesses of hunger, lust, anger, and violence.

15 | 2025-01-23#

mass produced pamphlet found in Kas men belongings

PAMPHLET

In a society built around a grand Celestial Order that is constantly revised and reinterpreted, the only thing that cannot change, the only thing that must not change, is the system itself.

Every revision, every reinterpretation takes place within a rigid framework of social stratification, the core of your society: Utility as an organizing principle. If one could step outside the system, he could recognize it for what it is: A prison. Worse than that, it is a prison full of willing prisoners. And not only are you prisoners within the system, you are also prisoners within your own bodies. Moonborn or Sunborn, men or women, you are trapped inside your laws and customs. The Emperor and his Astrologis built the lock and the Ministers hold the key, but most of you are unaware that you are locked in.

Make no mistake, your life is mapped out in front of you, as clear as the grooves in a Dragon’s scaled. You cannot choose a profession more than the sun could choose to stop orbiting the sky. A man born under the constallation of the Laborer cannot become a soldier more than a rock acquire a consciousness.
In denying your ability to choose a path of your own choosing, both the astrologers and the ministers say that they act in your own best interests. They have a responsibility, they say to ensure that you make best use of your God-given life. If your horoscope is found to be a soldier, it is because the gods know you need more soldiers. To deviate from your function with things like learning to dance, is to risk invoking their wrath.

In truth, it’s about control. A multi-skilled population is an empowered population. If you reject your horoscope, what’s next ? Would you reject your caste ? Would you reject your goverment ?
The Astrologers do not rely solely on theology when rebutting arguments for change. Working outside the boundaries of millennia old beliefs would be confusing. Imagine being a soldier led by a female general. You would question their competence, and they extend the same question to the scribes. “Would you feel comfortable working alongside a male ?” And it is true. People would be unnerved- at first. But it’s the priesthood itself, enabled by the ministers, that have given rise to this culture of suspicion. And they have done so deliberately, because they have usurped it for their own ends. It fosters first and foremost, division.

And that’s why when you see a stranger, you don’t think “What are they like ?”, but “What are they are for ?” . You don’t think “What are their hopes, dreams, aspirations ?”, but “What do they do”, and then “Where are they positioned in relation to me ? Are they above, or below me ?”. The more walls you can put in between people after all, the easier it is to contain them.

Even if one is an ardent believer in the Celestial Order, they have to ask themselves this: Who decides that order ? And then: Why should there be an order ? And that is the question they fear the most, because they know that their system would collapse if we arrived at the answer: There shouldn’t be.

Be happy in your work, they say, for it enriches you. Be grateful to your betters, for they want the best of you. Be thankful for the system, for it protects you. I say enough. Reject your work. Resist the system. And your “betters” ? You have none. We are all equal. And we have a right to decide how to live our lives.

From The Old World’s Demise, We Rise.

Join Kas’ Rebellion.

Drowned#

BAND: the Drowned Crew
as witnessed by the Bonepiper Bosun

There is darkness.
And peace.
For a moment.

Then they are replaced by a pulling,
a pain too strong to resist.

And then… by emptiness.

The cold.
It burns.

Dignity in death,
a notion contrived by fools.
An assertion of lies,
told by humans to comfort themselves,
in the face of an immutable force.
But not I.
By sacrificing my soul, I have conquered death,
and will live on past all those who tried to destroy me.
Until the end of time.
Bitter and alone.
For what purpose?

8.8.4817
My loyal Delishart,

I have been informed of Ludvig’s cruel and unfortunate death in the hands of ravaging pirates. It is with great sadness that I have to part ways with such a great wizard, friend and trusted servant.

Upon these circumstances, I name thee Overseer of Marjalat. It is now on your hands to find those responsible and make them pay. I believe that their concerning appearances will multiply, so you have to reinforce the city to face such danger.

I know there is unrest in the city. Baron Stephan, as well as my daughter need firm and bold guidance in these troubled times. I place upon your able hands this duty and request frequent updates of the situation in town.

Duke Naldar Ascado

20.8.4817

Dear Overseer Delishart,

I write in despair. My son is abducted! Last night, a dark figure appeared, impossible to recognise, took the by and left. He seem to have disappeared. I searched the manor and the upper city with the guards, but to no avail. They continue to search at this moment.

I wouldn’t send this letter if I wasn’t certain. I failed you, once again but most importantly, I failed myself. Maybe I am not worthy of your trust anymore. It seems this city is cursed and me as its Baron is a terrible idea. I beg you, dear friend, find my son! I put my judgement to your authority, as well as the Duke’s. If you decide I am to step down, I will do so without hesitation.

Thank you for your patience,

Baron Stephan Fonz

9.9.4817

My loyal Overseer Delishart,

I received your letters, yet had no time to respond, for my duties have been demanding as of late. Your rebuilding and scouting effords are to be commended. I am aware of numerous pirate activity on the south and west of the island, and was just informed that WNS Dolphin and WNS Violet Pearl were lost, as well as most of their crew.

By now, it is certain that they plan a mass scale invasion of Whistor. I know not if Della is behind the ploy, but I suspect losing Sjemhe’s ships wasn’t part of her plan. She wouldn’t tolerate it, so we have to assume she is indifferent or even also a target of these new attacks.

I lost contact with one of my spies I sent in Krantalli. I now there was a pirate gathering there with hundreds of ships, so we have to assume the next part of their plan is imminent. I expect your ships to be at the ready. Intensify the scouting routes and put the militia on maximum alert.

Duke Naldar Ascado

5th FIGURE (CLEARLY ELVEN)
No one can deny that he achieved the unachievable and he was able to do that only by aiming for the unimaginable. You all can hate me as much as you want, but the truth is that he knew the risks, he did what he believed was right, and as much as you want to blame me for this conclusion, deep down you all know it was his choice. CHANDOR

Stop talking. No one has the right to abuse his name and by no means keep pursuing this pointless cause. His addiction to prevent one imaginary threat was his undoing. When we had the chance we all followed him instead of stopping him, but you pushed him further. The rest is history. We should celebrate his life instead of fixating on his death.

Kemil   folk#

the Elemental Master

Ο Kemil, γεννημένος από γονείς “στελέχη” της Kληρονομιάς του Ashiro, έγινε και αυτός μέλος σε πολύ μικρή ηλικία.
Πολύ νεαρός άρχισε να διαβάζει μαγεία και είχε βάλει στόχο της ζωής του να ξεκλειδώσει τα μυστικά της μαγείας του Ashiro και να καταφέρει να μεταφράσει όσες κρυπτογραφημένες γραφές είχε αφήσει πίσω, αφού όπως έλεγε “Είμαι πολύ ταλαντούχος στην μαγεία ακόμα και για ξωτικό, αν δεν τα καταφέρω εγώ, τότε ποιος;”.
Ο Kemil ταξίδεψε πολύ και είχε δύσκολες περιπέτειες που ακόμα και οι πιο ικανοί πολεμιστές της ιστορίας δεν θα υποτιμούσαν. Ήταν 253 χρονών όταν το 4219, όταν όλοι οι συνταξιδιώτες του έδωσαν την ζωή τους όσο αυτός τελείωνε ένα ξόρκι για να κλειδώσει έναν από τους ανώτερους Δαίμονες πίσω στην άβυσσο, μετά βίας κατάφερε να το τελειώσει με την ζωή του. Μετά από αυτό εγκαταστάθηκε μόνιμα στον Warm ή όπως αλλιώς είναι γνωστός “ο ποταμός που δεν θα έπρεπε να υπάρχει”, μάλλον επειδή πίστευε πως ο Warm ήταν δημιουργία του Ashiro και πως εκεί θα ήταν κατάλληλο μέρος να μελετήσει την μαγεία και τις μαγικές γραφές που έχει αφήσει πίσω “το πιο Σπουδαίο Ξωτικό”.
Μια ιδέα του είχε καρφωθεί στο μυαλό, πως αφού οι Στοιχειακοί Άρχοντες ήταν οι ισχυρότεροι θεοί ίσως αυτοί να ήταν που είχαν ευλογήσει τον Ashiro, πως ίσως η μελέτη της στοιχειακής μαγείας ήταν το κλειδί στην αποκρυπτογράφηση των μυστικών γραφών. Μετά από σχεδόν δύο αιώνες δημιούργησε ένα ξόρκι που άλλαζε θεμελιακά ένα στοιχείο σε ένα άλλο (αν και πολλοί λένε πως αποκρυπτογράφησε έναν πάπυρο του Ashiro που είχε μέσα αυτό το ξόρκι) και πάνω σε αυτό έχουν βασιστεί πολλά ξόρκια στοιχειακής μαγείας.

  • κοντρα με Chandor, ο οποιος κατα τα λεγομενα του ερχοτανε να τον σταματησει στο εργαστηριο του κοντα στον ποταμο (μελος της Κληρονομια του Ashiro γαρ και μαλλον ουτε καν μονο γι αυτο)

Κληρονομια του Ashiro   faction#

Μετά τον Μεγάλο Πόλεμο του Βορρά, στο Νέο Oventhart […] όλες οι φυλές που ενυπήρχαν στο Βασίλειο απαιτούσαν να έχουν ίσα δικαιώματα με τα ξωτικά.
Όταν ο στρατός του Oventhart έφυγε στον νότο για τον Πόλεμο των Παγετώνων, δεν υπήρχε άλλη λύση από το να παραχωρηθούν μερικά από αυτά που ζητούσαν οι άλλες φυλές.
Η εφαρμογή αυτού του πράγματος, είχε ένα μοιραίο λάθος. Δεν διάλυσαν μια ιδέα που είχε χαρακτεί βαθιά μέσα στα ξωτικά της περιοχής. Ότι αυτά είναι ανώτερη φυλή και ότι δικαιούνται περισσότερα. Η ιδέα αυτή μέσα στο πέρασμα των αιώνων πήρε σώμα. Αυτό είναι η Κληρονομιά του Ashiro.
Δεν υπάρχει τίποτα πιο επικίνδυνο για το Oventhart σήμερα.
— Chandor, ο Ξένος

Gruhan completes ritual Mordrydd and Knut

Once per day, each bonded creature can use a 2-action activity, which has the concentrate trait, to learn the present state of the other bonded creature. The creature knows the other creature’s direction and distance and any conditions affecting them. Both of the participants can cast message as a divine innate spell at will, but can only target the other participant.

4816-11-17#

02 | 2017-03-05 | the Low Ghostwoods#

wherein Rhägar, Olsarthis, Ternath and Ulsath wander along eerie forests and windy coastlines; salvage gear off shipwrecks while driving off a goblin wrecker band; encounter a mysterious cliffside shrine holding something sinister within; converse with goofy goblin fishermen; battle sneaky grindylows and deadly draugr in knee-deep water alongside orc warrior castaways; meet Fuurindel; and are accepted into the goblin stronghold of the Winter Legion for negotiations, hoping to acquire a certain map from the goblins, which Olsarthis covertly steals while Fuurindel duels the goblin champion.

4816-11-21#

03 | 2016-03-08 | the Blood Moon#

wherein the castaways retrieve Orok’s greatblade and make their way through the woods to the northwest; are ambushed by a kobold patrol during the night; trail along a great chasm; ascend uphill along an old marked path and encounter a battlefield with disoriented ogre stragglers, the tackling of which results in a desperate last stand and Olsarthis’ death; they afterwards hike to Fort Fuyuhan where the shadows of the Sennarii are finally relieved of their duty and Olsarthis is resurrected upon the Menhir of the Fallen by the touch of the Bloodmist.

4816-11-23#

04 | 2017-03-14 | the Trek Eastwards#

wherein the six of them skirt the borders of the ogres’ hills and delve into the misty forests, encountering trolls and amphiptera before entering the Great Crater, an ancient battlefield whereupon the bones of the fallen soldiers rise to swarm them; narrowly escaping death by blood mist or skeleton claw, they continue eastwards through the Low Marshes, struggling against the onslaught of malaria and swarms of blood-sucking stirges, until at last the swamp lays behind them, and the Carven Keep ahead.

4816-11-28#

05 | 2017-05-04 | the Goblin Battlefield#

wherein the ragtag band of survivors lick their wounds for two days before finally descending into the carven crater where they discover a recent battle has taken place, hidden from sight or hearing, between goblins and hobgoblins; after disposing of two stray junk golems, they enter the Carven Keep where they are confronted by a mysterious reptilian guardian.

4816-11-30#

06 | 2017-05-27 | the Halls of Carven Keep#

wherein the band of six has naught but to delve deeper into the Carven Keep after resolving the tensions with the Hypnalis guardian, fighting their way through twisted undead monks, goblin-headed snakes, voracious blightspawn, and a vicious grimslake.

07 | 2017-06-03 | the Beginning#

wherein Olsarthis is paralyzed by Deathweb spiderlings and Rhägar saves Ternath from the maw of a Lurking Ray with an improbable stunt as the taint of the Harrowmist closes in to his heart; wherein the slumbering Chosen and the light of day are seen, a Cassissian angel named Phaenniel shows the path to salvation and Fuu accepts the petrifying curse that lays it open; wherein Rhägar charges one last time to meet death on his terms while his comrades escape the Carven Keep.

08 | 2017-07-07 | the Ascent#

wherein the ragtag band of misfits make the long trek uphill, towards the red beacon atop the mountain, through eerie woods and a desolate haunted village; across crumbling pathways and suspended rope bridges, their frustration and distrust with one another growing after the unprovoked killing of an Owb and a Lunar Naga, set aside to narrowly escape an encounter with a deadly Moonbeast.

4816-12-01#

09 | 2017-07-10 | the Harrow#

wherein the tormented adventurers enter the bowel of the mountain at the southeastern edge of Selanaar and traverse dwarven pathways in volcanic caverns past a Guecubu and a Shard Slag until they unexpectedly meet with more survivors, and follow them to the Castle of Lohrtirion at the top of the mountain where the remaining members of the Second Sennarii Expedition are bracing for the Seventh Dawn; everything becomes clear, and a long night passes.
As the hordes of Shadow mass on the castle walls, the party initiates a ritual upon the mountain lake but are interrupted by the arrival of the mighty underworld dragon Fang Zhin; the intense fight ends with the dragon’s death upon the sword of Yuugen while the expedition Commander sacrifices himself so that the ritual might be completed; and as the Haruspex of Voolthow treads on the castle grounds, the now-freed Prophetess under the Mountain comes forth to meet him.
The Comms Tower bursts with signalling light as the survivors race to the ground of their last stand; the undead and the Harrowmist close in; what small hope yet remained fades; and upon the first rays of daybreak, the eagles arrive.

Angaeradh stargazes

The race for the Blade of Dusk is afoot under the City of Shade.
The shadow of a puppetmaster is revealed; a fallen angel eternally bleeding from his forehead who seeks retribution against the elven god protector.
Two old friends, the master abjurer’s pupils, meet by a keep overgrown, with swords and staves in hand. Regretful words are exchanged afore their duel. Faenyss, the mage who would end magic, walks away driven to see this story end; Bjorg, the chosen of Azuth, is slain.
The Triumvirate forces push on in the last stretch of their quest. The Harper spies mourn in silence, and watch for their moment.
The menacing ambition of the Shadovar looms over them all.
